<div class="entry-details">
<header class="entry-header">
<div class="entry-meta"> <span class="entry-category"><a href="https://www.romeing.it/category/events_in_rome/exhibitions_in_rome/" rel="category tag">Exhibitions</a>, <a href="https://www.romeing.it/category/events_in_rome/" rel="category tag">Events in Rome</a></span></div><h2 class="entry-title">
<a href="https://www.romeing.it/on-the-spiritual-matter-of-art-exhibition-rome/">On the Spiritual Matter of Art at MAXXI</a>
</h2>
<span class="written-by">by</span>
<span class="author">
<a href="https://www.romeing.it/author/ciarapignatelli/" rel="author">
ciarapignatelli                                        </a>
</span>
</header>
<div class="entry-summary"> <p>An exhibition where 19 artists explore the dichotomy between the material and spiritual dimension, using art to elevate the spirit above the mundane. </p></div></div></div></article></div><div class="grid-6 column-right"><div class="grids grid-layout featured-carousel slick-initialized slick-slider slick-vertical"><div aria-live="polite" class="slick-list draggable" style="height: 600px;"><div class="slick-track" role="listbox" style="opacity: 1; height: 900px; transform: translate3d(0px, 0px, 0px);"><div aria-describedby="slick-slide20" aria-hidden="false" class="slick-slide slick-current slick-active" data-slick-index="0" role="option" style="width: 360px;" tabindex="-1"><div><article class="grid-6 post-64868 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-work-study-in-rome category-hot-topics post-item post-even" style="width: 100%; display: inline-block;">
<div class="post-item-inner">
<figure class="entry-image">
<a href="https://www.romeing.it/the-secret-to-landing-your-dream-job/" tabindex="0">
<img alt="Temple Rome Entry Year Program" class="attachment-rectangle-size-small size-rectangle-size-small wp-post-image" height="180" sizes="(max-width: 270px) 100vw, 270px" src="https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-270x180.jpg" srcset="https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-270x180.jpg 270w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-300x200.jpg 300w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-768x512.jpg 768w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-1024x683.jpg 1024w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-370x247.jpg 370w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-570x380.jpg 570w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-e1570523029637.jpg 770w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-1170x780.jpg 1170w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-870x580.jpg 870w, https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Temple-Rome-Entry-Year-Program-1320x880.jpg 1320w" width="270"/> </a>
</figure>
<div class="entry-details">

<article class="grid-4 post-44813 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-masters-program-in-rome category-features tag-study-abroad-in-rome tag-master tag-mba post-item post-even" id="postid-44813">

<a href="https://www.romeing.it/jackie-o-restaurant-via-veneto-rome/" rel="bookmark">The Dolce Vita at Via Veneto’s iconic Jackie O’ restaurant</a>

<a href="https://www.romeing.it/ex-galleria-monti-bar-rome/" rel="bookmark">Ex Galleria Monti: where cocktails &amp; art meet</a>
</h2>

<article class="grid-4 post-64577 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-rome-nightlife-guide category-bars_clubs_rome category-best-of-rome tag-bars_clubs_rome tag-bars tag-cocktail-bar tag-rome-nightlife-guide tag-speakeasy tag-drinking tag-mixology post-item post-even" id="postid-64577">

<div class="widget widget_ti-about-site" id="ti-about-site-2"><h3>About Us</h3><div class="social-center"><p>This insider guide on how to live like a local is the first free press, event and lifestyle magazine devoted to Rome and written entirely in English!<br/> Romeing is a magazine for tourists and expats visiting or living in Rome, including classifieds, an event calendar and feature articles written by locals with secret insight for your vacation or for those considering moving to live in Italy. A free, pocket-sized guide to the best locations, with a daily listing of the latest and most diverse cultural and lifestyle events in and around Rome. This is an essential information tool that allows foreigners of every age and background to live Rome to the fullest and discover the top things to see and to do in Rome.</p></div><ul class="social social-center clearfix"><li><a aria-hidden="true" class="icomoon-twitter" href="https://twitter.com/romeing" target="_blank"></a></li><li><a aria-hidden="true" class="icomoon-facebook" href="https://www.facebook.com/Romeing.it" target="_blank"></a></li><li><a aria-hidden="true" class="icomoon-google-plus" href="https://plus.google.com/+RomeingIt" target="_blank"></a></li><li><a aria-hidden="true" class="icomoon-instagram" href="http://instagram.com/romeing_magazine" target="_blank"></a></li></ul></div><div class="widget widget_ti_code_banner" id="ti_code_banner-2"> <img alt="logo_romeing_footer" src="https://www.romeing.it/wp-content/themes/latest/images/logo_romeing_bianco_footer.png"/></div></div><div class="widget-area widget-area-2" role="complementary">
